- **inv-callstack**: ある関数/モジュールを「誰が呼んでいるか」を呼び出し元へ遡って木構造で示す手順。責務の置き場所を決めたい・引数や API を消せるか知りたい・変更の影響範囲を測りたいときに使う。順方向(何を呼ぶか)は /callstack で、図の形は共通 ([Mikanixonable/dive-into-tepui](https://github.com/Mikanixonable/dive-into-tepui/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/inv-callstack))
- **inv-runner**: Python Invoke task automation for DTX website. This skill should be used when running repo commands, backend tests via Docker, database migrations, updating invoke tasks, Docker operations, or workflow automation. - **invalidate-security-issue**: Close an `&lt;tracker&gt;` tracking issue as invalid: apply the `invalid` label, remove the scope label, post a short closing comment, archive the item from the project board, and — for trackers imported from `&lt;security-list&gt;` — draft a polite-but-firm reply to the reporter on the original Gmail thread explaining the team's reasoning (extracted from the tracker's discussion). For trackers opened via `import-security-issue-from-pr`, the email-draft step is skipped per the *no outreach to the PR author* rule of that skill. - **invalidate-the-lore**: Harvest a team's beliefs about why users behave, convert each into a testable claim, and run the cheapest disproving test — building an append-only lore ledger of confirmed/busted/undecidable verdicts. Use when roadmap arguments run on gut feeling, before any big bet built on an untested assumption, on a quarterly hygiene cadence, or when someone says "users churn because…" without a link attached. ([zakelfassi/skdd-commons](https://github.com/zakelfassi/skdd-commons/tree/HEAD/packs/2026-07-growth-circle/invalidate-the-lore))

- **invalidauthenticationtoken-on-azure-co-3879f3c8f293f16d**: Fixes Azure Cost Management API calls failing with InvalidAuthenticationToken. - **invalidbatch-salesforce-bulk-api-batch-7cace626d1f8e91d**: Fixes Salesforce Bulk API InvalidBatch: the batch is in a state that rejects new data, usually because the job closed or the batch already completed. Check job state and open a fresh job. Use on Bulk API 1.0 batch errors. Not for Bulk API 2.0 ingest jobs. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/invalidbatch-salesforce-bulk-api-batch-7cace626d1f8e91d))
